Abstract: Video semantic search in densely crowded scenes remains a challenging task due to visual encoders tendency to prioritize salient foreground regions while neglecting contextually important, background areas. We propose an Inverse Attention Embedding mechanism that explicitly captures and highlights these overlooked regions. By combining inverse attention embeddings with traditional visual embeddings, our method significantly enhances semantic retrieval performance without additional training. Initial experiments and ablation studies demonstrate promising improvements over existing approaches in recall for video semantic search in crowded environments.

</details>


### [73] [Spark3R: Asymmetric Token Reduction Makes Fast Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06270)
*Zecheng Tang,Jiaye Fu,Qiankun Gao,Haijie Li,Yanmin Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spark3R是一种无需训练的加速框架，针对前馈式3D重建模型中的Vision Transformer，通过解耦查询令牌和键值令牌的压缩策略（查询令牌使用组内令牌合并，键值令牌使用轻量令牌剪枝），并自适应调整各层的键值缩减因子，在保持重建质量的同时，对1000帧视频输入实现高达28倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D重建模型在处理数百至数千帧的视频输入时，全局注意力层的二次计算代价导致扩展困难。现有令牌合并方法对查询令牌和键值令牌采用统一缩减策略，忽略了二者在3D重建中功能角色的差异。

Method: 提出Spark3R框架：(1) 解耦查询令牌与键值令牌的压缩，分别为二者分配不同的缩减因子；(2) 查询令牌采用组内令牌合并，键值令牌采用轻量令牌剪枝；(3) 自适应调整各层键值缩减因子以优化质量-效率权衡。该框架无需重新训练，可直接集成到多个预训练模型中。

Result: 在1000帧输入上实现高达28倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的重建质量，并成功集成到VGGT、π³和Depth-Anything-3等多个预训练模型中。

Conclusion: 通过区分查询令牌和键值令牌在3D重建中的不同角色并采用差异化的压缩策略，Spark3R有效解决了前馈式3D重建模型向视频输入扩展时的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models based on Vision Transformers can directly estimate scene geometry and camera poses from a small set of input images, but scaling them to video inputs with hundreds or thousands of frames remains challenging due to the quadratic cost of global attention layers. Recent token-merging methods accelerate these models by compressing the token sequence within the global attention layers, but they apply a uniform reduction to query tokens and key-value tokens, ignoring their functionally distinct roles in 3D reconstruction. In this work, we identify a key property of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models: query tokens encode view-specific geometric requests and are sensitive to compression, while key-value tokens represent shared scene context and tolerate aggressive compression. Guided by this insight, we propose Spark3R, a training-free acceleration framework that decouples the compression of query tokens and key-value tokens by assigning distinct reduction factors, with intra-group token merging applied to query tokens and lightweight token pruning to key-value tokens. Additionally, Spark3R adaptively adjusts the key-value reduction factor across layers, further improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. As a plug-and-play framework requiring no retraining, Spark3R integrates directly into multiple pretrained feed-forward 3D reconstruction models, including VGGT, $π^3$, and Depth-Anything-3, and achieves up to $28\times$ speedup on 1,000-frame inputs while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [74] [On-Orbit Real-Time Wildfire Detection Under On-Board Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06273)
*Matthias Rötzer,Veronika Pörtge,Martin Ickerott,Jayendra Praveen Kumar Chorapalli,Dimitri Scheftelowitsch,Max Bereczky,Dmitry Rashkovetsky,Sai Manoj Appalla,Julia Gottfriedsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套部署在九颗商业热红外卫星星座上的星上野火检测系统，在亚MB模型、亚150ms推理延迟及端到端10分钟预警链路的苛刻约束下运行。该系统基于未校准的200米分辨率中波红外单波段图像，采用DenseMAE自监督预训练方法，在极端类别不平衡条件下实现了0.699 AP和0.744 Fire-F1，优于监督基线，且最快模型仅0.52 MB、延迟65.34 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流野火监测系统（如MODIS、VIIRS）依赖上下文热阈值管道，无法有效处理亚像素或单像素热异常及极端类别不平衡问题，且缺乏面向星上边缘部署的轻量级深度学习方案。旨在满足卫星星座在轨实时检测的严苛资源与延迟约束。

Method: 采用DenseMAE（密集掩码自编码）及DenseMAE+EMA蒸馏变体进行自监督表征学习，在专有的九卫星MWIR数据集上预训练，随后通过线性探测和全分布像素级平均精度评估，并压缩为TensorRT FP16模型部署于NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX。

Result: 最快SSL预训练模型在0.52 MB、65.34 ms延迟下达到0.640 AP和0.69 Fire-F1；最佳配置在亚1 MB下达到0.699 AP和0.744 Fire-F1，超越监督基线（0.650 AP）。

Conclusion: DenseMAE自监督预训练能够在极端资源约束下生成紧凑且高性能的下游野火检测模型，为星上实时热异常检测提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: We present a deployed system for on-orbit wildfire detection aboard a nine-satellite commercial thermal infrared constellation, operating under demanding joint constraints: sub-megabyte model footprint, sub-150 ms per-batch TensorRT FP16 inference on an NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX, and an end-to-end alert pipeline targeting under 10 minutes from satellite overpass to fire event communication. The system operates on uncalibrated mid-wave infrared (MWIR) single-band imagery at 200 m ground sampling distance, where fires frequently appear as sub-pixel or single-pixel thermal anomalies under extreme class imbalance -- challenges not addressed by the contextual thermal-thresholding pipelines (MODIS, VIIRS) that currently dominate operational fire monitoring. We present an empirical study of lightweight dense representation learning for this regime using a proprietary nine-satellite MWIR dataset. We compare dense masked autoencoding (DenseMAE) and a hybrid DenseMAE+EMA (exponential moving average) distillation variant, and evaluate representations via linear probing and full-distribution pixel-level average precision (AP) under extreme class imbalance. DenseMAE pretraining enables compact downstream models on the latency-accuracy Pareto frontier: our fastest SSL-pretrained model achieves 0.640 test AP and 0.69 event-level Fire-F1 with 65.34 ms latency per batch and a 0.52 MB engine, without pruning or compression. The best configuration reaches 0.699 AP and 0.744 Fire-F1 below 1 MB, outperforming a supervised baseline (0.650 AP) under comparable constraints.

</details>


### [75] [Eulerian Motion Guidance: Robust Image Animation via Bidirectional Geometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06280)
*Thong Nguyen,Khoi M. Le,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Luu Anh Tuan,See-Kiong Ng,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于欧拉运动场（相邻帧光流）的图像动画方法，通过双向几何一致性机制解决遮挡区域的漂移伪影，相比基于参考帧的方法，实现了更快的训练、更好的时间一致性和更少的动态伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控图像动画框架通常依赖拉格朗日运动引导（相对于初始帧的光流估计），这种方法存在监督误差累积和训练并行化受限的问题。

Method: 1) 使用相邻帧欧拉运动场替代初始帧参考的运动引导，实现并行化训练和有界误差监督；2) 引入双向几何一致性机制，通过前向-后向循环检查数学识别并掩蔽遮挡区域，防止模型学习错误的目标扭曲。

Result: 实验表明该方法加速了训练过程，保持了时间一致性，并相比基于参考帧的基线方法减少了动态伪影。

Conclusion: 使用局部相邻帧运动监督配合双向一致性约束，能够更高效地训练图像动画扩散模型，有效缓解漂移问题。

Abstract: Recent advancements in image animation have utilized diffusion models to breathe life into static images. However, existing controllable frameworks typically rely on Lagrangian motion guidance, where optical flow is estimated relative to the initial frame. This paper revisits the same optical-flow primitive through a more local supervision design: we use adjacent-frame Eulerian motion fields to guide generation, where the motion signal always describes a short temporal hop. This shift enables parallelized training and provides bounded-error supervision throughout the generation process. To mitigate the drift artifacts common in adjacent frame generation, we introduce a Bidirectional Geometric Consistency mechanism, which computes a forward-backward cycle check to mathematically identify and mask occluded regions, preventing the model from learning incorrect warping objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach accelerates training, preserves temporal coherence, and reduces dynamic artifacts compared to reference-based baselines.

</details>


### [76] [Render, Don't Decode: Weight-Space World Models with Latent Structural Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06298)
*Roussel Desmond Nzoyem,Mauro Comi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA是一种基于隐式神经表示（INR）的世界建模框架，将系统状态编码为辅助INR的权重和偏置，通过解析渲染消除解码器瓶颈，实现紧凑、可移植、零样本超分辨率，并能在无辅助损失下解耦场景结构（背景、前景、帧间运动），支持可控预测和内容/动力学编辑，在单消费级GPU上以约4000万参数运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型将原始像素编码为不透明潜空间并依赖重型解码器进行重建，导致计算成本高且缺乏可解释性。作者提出用结构化表示替代传统潜空间，以解决解码器瓶颈和可解释性不足的问题。

Method: 将系统状态表示为辅助坐标基隐式神经表示（INR）的权重和偏置；通过解析渲染（analytical rendering）实现重建，消除解码器瓶颈；采用动作匹配目标（action-matching objective）将模型蒸馏为上下文相关的视频生成器；无需辅助损失或对抗目标即可实现场景成分（背景、前景、帧间运动）的解耦。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的数据集上实现了强可控预测；在单消费级GPU上以约4000万参数运行；支持零样本超分辨率；能够解耦并编辑场景的结构成分（内容或动力学）。

Conclusion: 基于INR的结构化表示不仅增强了对潜动力学的理解，还为沉浸式和可定制的虚拟体验铺平了道路，代表了世界建模从黑箱潜空间向可解释结构化表示转变的重要方向。

Abstract: Training world models on vast quantities of unlabelled videos is a critical step toward fully autonomous intelligence. However, the prevailing paradigm of encoding raw pixels into opaque latent spaces and relying on heavy decoders for reconstruction leaves these models computationally expensive and uninterpretable. We address this problem by introducing NOVA, a world modelling framework that represents the system state as the weights and biases of an auxiliary coordinate-based implicit neural representation (INR). This structured representation is analytically rendered, which eliminates the decoder bottleneck while conferring compactness, portability, and zero-shot super-resolution. Furthermore, like most latent action models, NOVA can be distilled into a context-dependent video generator via an action-matching objective. Surprisingly, without resorting to auxiliary losses or adversarial objectives, NOVA can disentangle structural scene components such as background, foreground, and inter-frame motion, enabling users to edit either content or dynamics without compromising the other. We validate our framework on several challenging datasets, achieving strong controllable forecasting while operating on a single consumer GPU at $\sim$40M parameters. Ultimately, structured representations like INRs not only enhance our understanding of latent dynamics but also pave the way for immersive and customisable virtual experiences.

</details>


### [77] [NavOne: One-Step Global Planning for Vision-Language Navigation on Top-Down Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06317)
*Dijia Zhan,Jinyi Li,Chenxi Zheng,Shaoyu Huang,Yong Li,Jie Tang,Xuemiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TD-VLN，将视觉语言导航重新定义为基于预建俯视图的单步全局路径规划问题，并构建R2R-TopDown数据集。提出NavOne框架，通过端到端前向传播直接在多模态地图上预测密集路径概率，实现8倍于现有地图方法、80倍于自我中心方法的规划速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法采用自我中心逐步范式，存在误差累积和效率低下的问题；基于预建地图的方法依赖增量更新记忆图或离散路径评分，限制了连续空间推理并造成离散瓶颈。

Method: 将导航重构为在预建俯视图上的单步全局路径规划问题；提出NavOne框架，包含Top-Down Map Fuser用于联合多模态地图表示，并扩展Attention Residuals实现空间感知深度混合，通过单次端到端前向传播直接预测密集路径概率。

Result: 在R2R-TopDown数据集上，NavOne在基于地图的VLN方法中达到最优性能，规划阶段速度比现有地图基线快8倍，比自我中心方法快80倍。

Conclusion: TD-VLN范式通过全局俯视图规划有效克服了逐步导航的误差累积和效率瓶颈，NavOne验证了端到端密集路径预测的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) methods typically adopt an egocentric, step-by-step paradigm, which struggles with error accumulation and limits efficiency. While recent approaches attempt to leverage pre-built environment maps, they often rely on incrementally updating memory graphs or scoring discrete path proposals, which restricts continuous spatial reasoning and creates discrete bottlenecks. We propose Top-Down VLN (TD-VLN), reformulating navigation as a one-step global path planning problem on pre-built top-down maps, supported by our newly constructed R2R-TopDown dataset. To solve this, we introduce NavOne, a unified framework that directly predicts dense path probabilities over multi-modal maps in a single end-to-end forward pass. NavOne features a Top-Down Map Fuser for joint multi-modal map representation, and extends Attention Residuals for spatial-aware depth mixing. Extensive experiments on R2R-TopDown show that NavOne achieves state-of-the-art performance among map-based VLN methods, with a planning-stage speedup of 8x over existing map-based baselines and 80x over egocentric methods, enabling highly efficient global navigation.

</details>


### [78] [TinyBayes: Closed-Form Bayesian Inference via Jacobi Prior for Real-Time Image Classification on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06333)
*Shouvik Sardar,Sourish Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyBayes框架，将闭式贝叶斯分类器（Jacobi-DMR）与移动级计算机视觉流水线（YOLOv8-Nano + MobileNetV3-Small）结合，在9.5 MB内实现可可病害检测，CPU推理<150ms，准确率78.7%，填补了边缘部署中不确定性量化与农业应用的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西非小农户依赖可可种植，但CSSVD和炭疽病造成严重减产；现有边缘部署的植物病害系统缺乏不确定性量化，而贝叶斯方法在边缘设备上主要关注硬件架构而非农业应用，两者之间存在空白。

Method: TinyBayes框架：YOLOv8-Nano（5.9 MB）进行病灶定位 → MobileNetV3-Small（3.5 MB）提取特征 → Jacobi-DMR分类器（13.5 KB，基于Jacobi先验的闭式非迭代贝叶斯估计），总模型9.5 MB，端到端CPU推理<150ms。

Result: 在Amini Cocoa Contamination Challenge数据集上达到78.7%准确率；与Random Forest、SVM、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、XGBoost、Jacobi-GP七种分类器对比，Jacobi-DMR在精度、模型大小和推理速度上取得最佳权衡；证明了Jacobi-DMR的渐近等价性、一致性、渐近正态性和偏差校正。

Conclusion: TinyBayes是首个将闭式贝叶斯分类器与移动级CV流水线结合的可可病害检测框架，在资源受限环境下实现了小模型、快推理、无需联网的有效部署，代码已开源。

Abstract: Cocoa (Theobroma cacao) is a critical cash crop for millions of smallholder farmers in West Africa, where Cocoa Swollen Shoot Virus Disease (CSSVD) and anthracnose cause devastating yield losses. Automated disease detection from leaf images is essential for early intervention, yet deploying such systems in resource-constrained settings demands models that are small, fast, and require no internet connectivity. Existing edge-deployable plant disease systems rely on end-to-end deep learning without uncertainty quantification, while Bayesian methods for edge devices focus on hardware-level inference architectures rather than agricultural applications. We bridge this gap with TinyBayes, the first framework to combine a closed-form Bayesian classifier with a mobile-grade computer vision pipeline for crop disease detection. Our pipeline uses YOLOv8-Nano (5.9 MB) for lesion localisation, MobileNetV3-Small (3.5 MB) for feature extraction, and the Jacobi prior; a Bayesian method that provides a closed form non-iterative estimators via projection, for the classification. The Jacobi-DMR (Distributed Multinomial Regression) classifier adds only 13.5 KB to the pipeline, bringing the total model size within 9.5 MB, while achieving 78.7% accuracy on the Amini Cocoa Contamination Challenge dataset and enabling end-to-end CPU inference under 150 ms per image. We benchmark against seven classifiers including Random Forest, SVM, Ridge, Lasso, Elastic Net, XGBoost, and Jacobi-GP, and demonstrate that the Jacobi-DMR offers the best trade-off between accuracy, model size, and inference speed for edge deployment. We have proved the asymptotic equivalence and consistency, asymptotic normality and the bias correction of Jacobi-DMR. All data and codes are available here: https://github.com/shouvik-sardar/TinyBayes

</details>


### [79] [Earth-o1: A Grid-free Observation-native Atmospheric World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06337)
*Junchao Gong,Kaiyi Xu,Wangxu Wei,Siwei Tu,Jingyi Xu,Zili Liu,Hang Fan,Zhiwang Zhou,Tao Han,Yi Xiao,Xinyu Gu,Zhangrui Li,Wenlong Zhang,Hao Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Yaqiang Wang,Lijing Cheng,Pierre Gentine,Wanli Ouyang,Feng Zhang,Zhe-Min Tan,Bowen Zhou,Fenghua Ling,Ben Fei,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Earth-o1是一种直接从未网格化的观测数据中学习地球系统连续三维物理演化的原生观测大气世界模型，无需传统数据同化或数值求解器，在回测评估中达到了与IFS可比的表面预报技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统建模框架将异质观测数据强制映射到预定义空间网格，限制了原始传感器数据的充分利用并造成严重的计算瓶颈，亟需一种能够直接利用原始观测数据建模大气动力学的新范式。

Method: Earth-o1采用连续、无网格的动力学场表示，直接从非网格化观测数据中学习地球系统的三维物理演化，整合多样化的传感器输入，自主推进大气状态在时空中的演化，无需显式数值求解器。

Result: 在回测评估中，Earth-o1的表面预报技能与欧洲中期天气预报中心（ECMWF）的IFS相当，证明了连续、观测驱动的世界模型能够达到与成熟物理框架相媲美的保真度。

Conclusion: Earth-o1代表了一类全新的、完全原生观测的地球物理模拟器——连续观测驱动世界模型，为构建可扩展的数据驱动地球数字孪生提供了基础。

Abstract: Despite the unprecedented volume of multimodal data provided by modern Earth observation systems, our ability to model atmospheric dynamics remains constrained. Traditional modeling frameworks force heterogeneous measurements into predefined spatial grids, inherently limiting the full exploitation of raw sensor data and creating severe computational bottlenecks. Here we present Earth-o1, an observation-native atmospheric world model that overcomes these structural limitations. Rather than relying on conventional atmospheric dynamical modeling systems or traditional data assimilation, Earth-o1 directly learns the continuous, three-dimensional physical evolution of the Earth system from ungridded observational data. By integrating diverse sensor inputs into a unified, grid-free dynamical field, the model autonomously advances the atmospheric state in space and time. We show that this fundamentally distinct paradigm enables direct, real-time forecasting and cross-sensor inference without the overhead of explicit numerical solvers. In hindcast evaluations, Earth-o1 achieves surface forecast skill comparable to the operational Integrated Forecasting System (IFS). These results establish that continuous, observation-driven world models -- a new class of fully observation-native geophysical simulators -- can match the fidelity of established physical frameworks, providing a scalable data-driven foundation for a digital twin of the Earth.

</details>


### [80] [SwiftI2V: Efficient High-Resolution Image-to-Video Generation via Conditional Segment-wise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06356)
*YaoYang Liu,Yuechen Zhang,Wenbo Li,Yufei Zhao,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SwiftI2V提出了一种高效的两阶段高分辨率图像到视频生成框架，首先生成低分辨率运动参考，再进行强图像条件约束的2K合成，通过条件分段生成和双向上下文交互，在保持输入保真度的同时将GPU时间减少202倍，并能在单张消费级GPU上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率I2V方法存在瓶颈：端到端模型内存和延迟过高；级联超分辨率方法因缺乏对输入图像的显式条件约束，容易产生细节幻觉并偏离输入图像的局部结构。亟需一种既能保证效率又能保持输入保真度的高分辨率I2V方案。

Method: SwiftI2V采用两阶段设计：1) 首先生成低分辨率运动参考以降低token成本和建模负担；2) 进行强图像条件约束的2K合成，由运动引导恢复输入保真细节。提出条件分段生成（CSG）方法，分段合成视频并限制每步token预算，同时在每个分段内采用双向上下文交互以改善跨段连贯性和输入保真度。

Result: 在VBench-I2V基准上，SwiftI2V在2K分辨率下达到与端到端基线可比的性能，同时将总GPU时间减少202倍，并能够在单张数据中心GPU（如H800）或消费级GPU（如RTX 4090）上实现实用的2K I2V生成。

Conclusion: SwiftI2V通过两阶段设计和条件分段生成策略，有效解决了高分辨率图像到视频生成中的效率与保真度权衡问题，实现了极低的计算开销下高质量的2K视频生成，显著降低了硬件门槛。

Abstract: High-resolution image-to-video (I2V) generation aims to synthesize realistic temporal dynamics while preserving fine-grained appearance details of the input image. At 2K resolution, it becomes extremely challenging, and existing solutions suffer from various weaknesses: 1) end-to-end models are often prohibitively expensive in memory and latency; 2) cascading low-resolution generation with a generic video super-resolution tends to hallucinate details and drift from input-specific local structures, since the super-resolution stage is not explicitly conditioned on the input image. To this end, we propose SwiftI2V, an efficient framework tailored for high-resolution I2V. Following the widely used two-stage design, it addresses the efficiency--fidelity dilemma by first generating a low-resolution motion reference to reduce token costs and ease the modeling burden, then performing a strongly image-conditioned 2K synthesis guided by the motion to recover input-faithful details with controlled overhead. Specifically, to make generation more scalable, SwiftI2V introduces Conditional Segment-wise Generation (CSG) to synthesize videos segment-by-segment with a bounded per-step token budget, and adopts bidirectional contextual interaction within each segment to improve cross-segment coherence and input fidelity. On VBench-I2V at 2K resolution, SwiftI2V achieves performance comparable to end-to-end baselines while reducing total GPU-time by 202x. Particularly, it enables practical 2K I2V generation on a single datacenter GPU (e.g., H800) or consumer GPU (e.g., RTX 4090).

</details>


### [81] [eXplaining to Learn (eX2L): Regularization Using Contrastive Visual Explanation Pairs for Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06368)
*Paulo Mario P. Medina,Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出eX2L框架，通过惩罚Grad-CAM激活图相似性来解耦混淆特征，在Spawrious基准上超越SOTA，分别提升5.49%（平均准确率）和10.90%（最差组准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布偏移缓解算法性能不一致，常无法超越ERM基线，且算法复杂度高、可解释性差，仅间接处理虚假相关。

Method: eX2L框架在训练期间惩罚主标签分类器与混淆分类器生成的Grad-CAM激活图之间的相似性，从而解耦混淆特征与分类器潜在表示。

Result: 在Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge上，AA达82.24%±3.87%，WGA达66.31%±8.73%，分别超过当前SOTA 5.49%和10.90%。

Conclusion: 通过显式在组级别解耦标签与 nuisance 属性，可实现功能性域不变性；eX2L在保持可解释性的同时取得竞争性能。

Abstract: Despite extensive research into mitigating distribution shifts, many existing algorithms yield inconsistent performance, often failing to outperform baseline Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, high algorithmic complexity frequently limits interpretability and offers only an indirect means of addressing spurious correlations. We propose eXplaining to Learn (eX2L): an interpretable, explanation-based framework that decorrelates confounding features from a classifier's latent representations during training. eX2L achieves this by penalizing the similarity between Grad-CAM activation maps generated by a primary label classifier and those from a concurrently trained confounder classifier. On the rigorous Spawrious Many-to-Many Hard Challenge benchmark, eX2L achieves an average accuracy (AA) of 82.24% +/- 3.87% and a worst-group accuracy (WGA) of 66.31% +/- 8.73%, outperforming the current state-of-the-art (SOTA) by 5.49% and 10.90%, respectively. Beyond its competitive performance, eX2L demonstrates that functional domain invariance can be achieved by explicitly decoupling label and nuisance attributes at the group level.

</details>


### [82] [Continuous-Time Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06376)
*Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Mengting Chen,Taihang Hu,Zhengrong Yue,Zihao Pan,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Bo Zheng,Yaxing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CDM将分布匹配蒸馏从离散时间扩展为连续时间优化，通过动态连续时间调度和连续时间对齐目标，在无需复杂辅助模块的情况下实现少步高质量图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散时间分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）仅在少数预定义离散时间步提供稀疏监督，且反向KL散度的模态追求特性导致视觉伪影和过度平滑，往往需要GAN或奖励模型等复杂辅助模块来恢复视觉保真度。

Method: 1）动态连续时间调度：用随机长度的动态连续调度替代固定离散调度，在采样轨迹的任意点而非固定锚点施加分布匹配约束。2）连续时间对齐目标：在通过学生速度场外推的潜变量上执行主动的离轨迹匹配，提升泛化能力并保留精细细节。

Result: 在SD3-Medium和Longcat-Image等不同架构上的实验表明，CDM在少步图像生成中提供了极具竞争力的视觉保真度，且无需依赖复杂的辅助目标。

Conclusion: 连续时间分布匹配蒸馏（CDM）通过将DMD从离散锚定迁移到连续优化，有效解决了离散DMD的视觉质量问题，为加速扩散模型提供了一种简洁高效的少步生成方案。

Abstract: Step distillation has become a leading technique for accelerating diffusion models, among which Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) and Consistency Distillation are two representative paradigms. While consistency methods enforce self-consistency along the full PF-ODE trajectory to steer it toward the clean data manifold, vanilla DMD relies on sparse supervision at a few predefined discrete timesteps. This restricted discrete-time formulation and mode-seeking nature of the reverse KL divergence tends to exhibit visual artifacts and over-smoothed outputs, often necessitating complex auxiliary modules -- such as GANs or reward models -- to restore visual fidelity. In this work, we introduce Continuous-Time Distribution Matching (CDM), migrating the DMD framework from discrete anchoring to continuous optimization for the first time. CDM achieves this through two continuous-time designs. First, we replace the fixed discrete schedule with a dynamic continuous schedule of random length, so that distribution matching is enforced at arbitrary points along sampling trajectories rather than only at a few fixed anchors. Second, we propose a continuous-time alignment objective that performs active off-trajectory matching on latents extrapolated via the student's velocity field, improving generalization and preserving fine visual details. Extensive experiments on different architectures, including SD3-Medium and Longcat-Image, demonstrate that CDM provides highly competitive visual fidelity for few-step image generation without relying on complex auxiliary objectives. Code is available at https://github.com/byliutao/cdm.

</details>


### [83] [Reconstruction or Semantics? What Makes a Latent Space Useful for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06388)
*Nilaksh,Saurav Jha,Artem Zholus,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种重建和语义编码器在动作条件潜扩散模型中构建世界模型的效果，发现视觉保真度不足以评估世界模型，语义潜空间（如V-JEPA 2.1、Web-DINO、SigLIP 2）在规划和策略性能上优于重建编码器（如VAE、Cosmos），主张采用语义潜空间作为机器人扩散世界模型的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着潜在扩散模型在机器人世界模型中的应用日益广泛，选择合适的潜空间成为关键问题。现有主流使用自动编码潜空间（如VAE），但近期研究表明预训练语义编码器可能更有优势。本文旨在系统评估不同潜空间对动作条件LDM世界模型的影响，并建立更全面的评估框架。

Method: 在BridgeV2数据集上采用固定协议，比较六种编码器（重建类：VAE、Cosmos；语义类：V-JEPA 2.1、Web-DINO、SigLIP 2等）训练世界模型变体，从视觉保真度、规划与下游策略性能、潜表示质量三个维度进行评估。

Result: 重建编码器在像素级得分上表现优异，但视觉保真度不足以选择世界模型。语义编码器在规划、策略性能和潜表示质量两个维度上全面优于重建编码器，其中V-JEPA 2.1在策略性能上综合最优。

Conclusion: 语义潜空间是构建策略相关的机器人扩散世界模型的更强基础，应取代当前以重建为导向的潜空间选择范式。

Abstract: World model-based policy evaluation is a practical proxy for testing real-world robot control by rolling out candidate actions in action-conditioned video diffusion models. As these models increasingly adopt latent diffusion modeling (LDM), choosing the right latent space becomes critical. While the status quo uses autoencoding latent spaces like VAEs that are primarily trained for pixel reconstruction, recent work suggests benefits from pretrained encoders with representation-aligned semantic latent spaces. We systematically evaluate these latent spaces for action-conditioned LDM by comparing six reconstruction and semantic encoders to train world model variants under a fixed protocol on BridgeV2 dataset, and show effective world model training in high-dimensional representation spaces with and without dimension compression. We then propose three axes to assess robotic world model performance: visual fidelity, planning and downstream policy performance, and latent representation quality. Our results show visual fidelity alone is insufficient for world model selection. While reconstruction encoders like VAE and Cosmos achieve strong pixel-level scores, semantic encoders such as V-JEPA 2.1 (strongest overall on policy), Web-DINO, and SigLIP 2 generally excel across the other two axes at all model scales. Our study advocates semantic latent space as stronger foundation for policy-relevant robotics diffusion world models.

</details>


### [84] [GeoStack: A Framework for Quasi-Abelian Knowledge Composition in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06477)
*Pranav Mantini,Shishir K. Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoStack是一个模块化框架，通过对适配器流形施加几何和结构约束，将独立训练的领域专家组合成统一模型，实现恒时推理复杂度(O(1))，并在多领域适应和类增量学习中显著缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视觉语言模型(VLMs)在跨多个领域或任务积累知识时出现的灾难性遗忘问题，实现知识的高效组合。

Method: 提出GeoStack框架，通过对适配器流形施加几何和结构约束来组合独立训练的领域专家，并利用权重折叠性质实现与专家数量无关的恒时推理复杂度。

Result: 在多领域适应和类增量学习实验中，GeoStack提供了高效的长时知识组合机制，显著缓解了灾难性遗忘，且推理复杂度恒为O(1)。

Conclusion: GeoStack通过几何约束的模块化专家组合策略，有效解决了VLM的知识组合与灾难性遗忘问题，同时保证了推理效率不随专家数量增长。

Abstract: We address the challenge of knowledge composition in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), where accumulating expertise across multiple domains or tasks typically leads to catastrophic forgetting. We introduce GeoStack (Geometric Stacking), a modular framework that allows independently trained domain experts to be composed into a unified model. By imposing geometric and structural constraints on the adapter manifold, GeoStack ensures the foundational knowledge of the base model is preserved. Furthermore, we mathematically demonstrate a weight-folding property that achieves constant-time inference complexity ($O(1)$), regardless of the number of integrated experts. Experimental results across multi-domain adaptation and class-incremental learning show that GeoStack provides an efficient mechanism for long-term knowledge composition while significantly mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Code is available at https://github.com/QuantitativeImagingLaboratory/GeoStack.

</details>


### [85] [3D MRI Image Pretraining via Controllable 2D Slice Navigation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06487)
*Yu Wang,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可控切片导航的自监督预训练方法，将3D MRI体积转换为可控制的2D渲染序列（类似视频动作序列），通过学习动作条件下的潜在动力学模型来表征解剖结构和空间信息，在多项下游任务中优于传统静态体素预训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督MRI预训练方法大多将扫描视为静态的切片、块或体素聚合，缺乏利用3D体积内在连续性结构的自监督信号。作者探索是否存在一种不同于掩码重建的、通过将3D体积转换为可控2D渲染序列（类似视频动作轨迹）的内在自监督形式。

Method: 将3D MRI体积转换为可控2D渲染序列：通过在连续位置、方向和尺度上渲染切片，将3D体积转换为密集的视频动作序列，其控制信号为动作轨迹。采用动作条件预训练目标：分词器编码切片观测，潜在动力学模型预测潜在特征的演化过程。

Result: 在代表性解剖和空间下游任务上，该方法优于标准的静态体素基线、仅分词器预训练以及无对齐动作的动力学变体，表明可控MRI切片导航是一种有效的互补预训练接口。

Conclusion: 可控MRI切片导航提供了一种新型的自监督信号，能够从大规模无标注MRI数据中学习解剖和空间表示，可作为现有静态预训练方法的有力补充。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining has become the mainstream approach for learning MRI representations from unlabeled scans. However, most existing objectives still treat each scan primarily as static aggregations of slices, patches or volumes. We ask whether there exists an intrinsic form of self-supervision signal that is different from reconstructing the masked patches, through transforming the 3D volumes into controllable 2D rendered sequences: by rendering slices at continuous positions, orientations, and scales, a 3D volume can be converted into dense video-action sequences whose controls are the action trajectories. We study this formulation with an action-conditioned pretraining objective, where a tokenizer encodes slice observations and a latent dynamics model predicts the evolution of latent features. Across representative anatomical and spatial downstream tasks, the proposed pretraining is evaluated against standard static-volume baselines, tokenizer-only pretraining, and dynamics variants without aligned actions. These results suggest that controllable MRI slice navigation provides a useful complementary pretraining interface for learning anatomical and spatial representations from large unlabeled MRI collections.

</details>


### [86] [MARBLE: Multi-Aspect Reward Balance for Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06507)
*Canyu Zhao,Hao Chen,Yunze Tong,Yu Qiao,Jiacheng Li,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MARBLE，一种梯度空间优化框架，通过维护独立优势估计器、计算每奖励策略梯度并利用二次规划求解统一更新方向，解决多奖励扩散模型微调中加权求和导致的样本级不匹配问题，在SD3.5 Medium上实现五个奖励维度同时提升，训练速度达基线0.97倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型多奖励对齐方法（训练单奖励专家模型、加权求和、顺序微调）无法生成统一模型或需大量手动调参，根本原因是加权求和聚合奖励存在样本级不匹配——大部分轨迹是专家样本，仅对特定奖励维度信息丰富，加权求和会稀释监督信号。

Method: MARBLE框架：1) 为每个奖励维护独立优势估计器；2) 计算每奖励的策略梯度；3) 通过求解二次规划问题将多个梯度和谐化为单一更新方向，无需手动调整奖励权重。进一步提出摊销公式利用DiffusionNFT损失的仿射结构，将每步计算成本从K+1次反向传播降至接近单奖励基线，并采用EMA平滑平衡系数以稳定更新。

Result: 在SD3.5 Medium上使用五个奖励进行实验，MARBLE同时提升所有五个奖励维度，将最差对齐奖励的梯度余弦从加权求和下80%小批量为负转变为持续为正，训练速度为基线训练的0.97倍。

Conclusion: 加权求和聚合多奖励导致样本级不匹配，MARBLE通过梯度空间优化有效解决该问题，实现多奖励联合训练的统一模型，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning has become the dominant approach for aligning diffusion models with human preferences. However, assessing images is intrinsically a multi-dimensional task, and multiple evaluation criteria need to be optimized simultaneously. Existing practice deal with multiple rewards by training one specialist model per reward, optimizing a weighted-sum reward $R(x)=\sum_k w_k R_k(x)$, or sequentially fine-tuning with a hand-crafted stage schedule. These approaches either fail to produce a unified model that can be jointly trained on all rewards or necessitates heavy manually tuned sequential training. We find that the failure stems from using a naive weighted-sum reward aggregation. This approach suffers from a sample-level mismatch because most rollouts are specialist samples, highly informative for certain reward dimensions but irrelevant for others; consequently, weighted summation dilutes their supervision. To address this issue, we propose MARBLE (Multi-Aspect Reward BaLancE), a gradient-space optimization framework that maintains independent advantage estimators for each reward, computes per-reward policy gradients, and harmonizes them into a single update direction without manually-tuned reward weighting, by solving a Quadratic Programming problem. We further propose an amortized formulation that exploits the affine structure of the loss used in DiffusionNFT, to reduce the per-step cost from K+1 backward passes to near single-reward baseline cost, together with EMA smoothing on the balancing coefficients to stabilize updates against transient single-batch fluctuations. On SD3.5 Medium with five rewards, MARBLE improves all five reward dimensions simultaneously, turns the worst-aligned reward's gradient cosine from negative under weighted summation in 80% of mini-batches to consistently positive, and runs at 0.97X the training speed of baseline training.

</details>


### [87] [FreeSpec: Training-Free Long Video Generation via Singular-Spectrum Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06509)
*Fangda Chen,Shanshan Zhao,Longrong Yang,Chuanfu Xu,Zhigang Luo,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FreeSpec，一种无需训练的频谱重建框架，通过奇异值分解将全局分支作为低秩频谱引导、局部分支作为高秩重建基础，解决长视频生成中的内容漂移、时间不一致和过度平滑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free的长视频扩展方法通过全局和局部分支组合提升时间一致性，但依赖预定义标准分解外观一致性和时间动态，在相机运动等外观与动作紧密耦合的场景下不可靠；且自注意力窗口扩大导致频谱能量集中于低秩方向，抑制了高秩空间细节和运动变化。

Method: 从奇异谱视角分析长视频扩展中的频谱集中问题；提出FreeSpec，利用奇异值分解将全局和局部特征分解，全局分支作为低秩频谱引导，局部分支作为高秩重建基，实现频谱级融合。

Result: 在Wan2.1和LTX-Video上验证，FreeSpec提升了长视频生成质量，尤其增强了时间动态性，同时保持了良好的视觉质量和时间一致性。

Conclusion: FreeSpec通过频谱级融合避免了刚性特征划分，在保留长程一致性的同时更好地保留了空间细节和时间动态，为长视频生成提供了一种有效的训练-free解决方案。

Abstract: Video diffusion models perform well in short-video synthesis, but their training-free extension to long videos often suffers from content drift, temporal inconsistency, and over-smoothed dynamics. Existing methods improve temporal consistency by combining a global branch with a local branch, but they often further decompose appearance consistency and temporal dynamics within each branch using predefined criteria. This assignment is unreliable when appearance and action progression are tightly coupled, such as in camera motion and sequential motion. We analyze the video temporal extension issue from a singular-spectrum perspective and show that enlarged self-attention windows induce spectral concentration: spectral energy becomes dominated by a few low-rank singular directions, preserving coarse structure but suppressing high-rank spatial details and motion-rich temporal variations. To mitigate this problem, we propose FreeSpec, a training-free spectral reconstruction framework for long-video generation. FreeSpec decomposes global and local features with singular value decomposition, and uses the global branch as low-rank spectral guidance and the local branch as a high-rank reconstruction basis. This spectrum-level fusion avoids the rigid feature partitioning of previous decomposition rules, preserving long-range consistency while better retaining spatial details and temporal dynamics. Experiments on Wan2.1 and LTX-Video demonstrate that FreeSpec improves long-video generation, especially for temporal dynamics, while maintaining strong visual quality and temporal consistency. Project demo: https://fdchen24.github.io/FreeSpec-Website/.

</details>


### [88] [DCR: Counterfactual Attractor Guidance for Rare Compositional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06512)
*Taewon Kang,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Default Completion Repulsion (DCR)框架，通过构建反事实吸引子和投影排斥机制，在无需重新训练的情况下抑制扩散模型中的默认补全偏差，从而提升稀有组合提示下的生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在处理训练数据中低频但合理的组合（如雪地沙滩、夜间彩虹）时，会坍塌到更常见的配置，现有引导机制无法显式建模这种竞争性倾向，导致无法有效防止生成失败。

Method: DCR通过放松稀有组合因子构建反事实吸引子，产生反映模型偏好补全的替代去噪轨迹；将目标轨迹与吸引子轨迹的差异定义为反事实漂移，并提出基于投影的排斥机制，移除与该漂移方向对齐的引导成分，从而抑制不需要的频繁补全。

Result: 在稀有组合提示的实验表明，DCR在保持视觉质量的同时提升了组合保真度；分析表明该方法能暴露并抵消模型内在偏差。

Conclusion: DCR无需训练或修改架构，完全在标准扩散采样过程中运行，为可控生成提供了超越显式约束强化的新视角。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate realistic visual content, yet often fail to produce rare but plausible compositions. When prompted with combinations that are valid but underrepresented in training data, such as a snowy beach or a rainbow at night, the generation process frequently collapses toward more common alternatives. We identify this failure mode as default completion bias, where denoising trajectories are implicitly attracted toward high-frequency semantic configurations. Existing guidance mechanisms do not explicitly model this competing tendency and therefore struggle to prevent such collapse. We introduce Default Completion Repulsion (DCR), a training-free framework that explicitly models and suppresses default completion behavior. DCR constructs a counterfactual attractor by relaxing the rare compositional factor while preserving surrounding semantics, inducing an alternative denoising trajectory reflecting the model's preferred completion. We define the discrepancy between target and attractor trajectories as a counterfactual drift, and propose a projection-based repulsion mechanism that removes guidance components aligned with this drift direction. This suppresses undesired frequent completions while preserving other semantic components. DCR operates entirely within the standard diffusion sampling process without retraining or architectural modification. Experiments on rare compositional prompts show that DCR improves compositional fidelity while maintaining visual quality. Our analysis further shows that the framework exposes and counteracts intrinsic model biases, offering a new perspective on controllable generation beyond explicit constraint enforcement.

</details>


### [89] [Sparkle: Realizing Lively Instruction-Guided Video Background Replacement via Decoupled Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06535)
*Ziyun Zeng,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频背景替换任务中高质量训练数据稀缺的问题，本文提出Sparkle数据集（~14万视频对）及Sparkle-Bench评估基准，通过解耦生成前景/背景引导的可扩展管道和严格质量过滤，显著提升了该任务的性能，超越现有所有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源视频编辑数据集主要集中于局部编辑或风格迁移，而背景替换任务因需要合成全新且时序一致的场景并保持前景-背景交互，大规模数据生成极具挑战性，导致该任务因缺乏高质量训练数据而探索不足，现有模型（如Kiwi-Edit）在背景替换上表现不佳，主推数据集OpenVE-3M常生成静态、不自然的背景。

Method: 设计了一个可扩展的合成管道，采用解耦方式分别生成前景和背景引导，并辅以严格的质量过滤；基于该管道构建了包含约14万视频对、涵盖五种常见背景更换主题的Sparkle数据集，以及最大规模的背景替换评估基准Sparkle-Bench。

Result: Sparkle数据集及在其上训练的模型在OpenVE-Bench和Sparkle-Bench上均显著优于所有现有基线模型。

Conclusion: 背景替换质量下降的根本原因是数据合成过程中缺乏精确的背景引导；通过解耦生成前景/背景引导的管道和严格质量过滤，可以有效构建高质量数据集，推动视频背景替换任务的发展。数据集、基准和模型已全部开源。

Abstract: In recent years, open-source efforts like Senorita-2M have propelled video editing toward natural language instruction. However, current publicly available datasets predominantly focus on local editing or style transfer, which largely preserve the original scene structure and are easier to scale. In contrast, Background Replacement, a task central to creative applications such as film production and advertising, requires synthesizing entirely new, temporally consistent scenes while maintaining accurate foreground-background interactions, making large-scale data generation significantly more challenging. Consequently, this complex task remains largely underexplored due to a scarcity of high-quality training data. This gap is evident in poorly performing state-of-the-art models, e.g., Kiwi-Edit, because the primary open-source dataset that contains this task, i.e., OpenVE-3M, frequently produces static, unnatural backgrounds. In this paper, we trace this quality degradation to a lack of precise background guidance during data synthesis. Accordingly, we design a scalable pipeline that generates foreground and background guidance in a decoupled manner with strict quality filtering. Building on this pipeline, we introduce Sparkle, a dataset of ~140K video pairs spanning five common background-change themes, alongside Sparkle-Bench, the largest evaluation benchmark tailored for background replacement to date. Experiments demonstrate that our dataset and the model trained on it achieve substantially better performance than all existing baselines on both OpenVE-Bench and Sparkle-Bench. Our proposed dataset, benchmark, and model are fully open-sourced at https://showlab.github.io/Sparkle/.

</details>


### [90] [MedHorizon: Towards Long-context Medical Video Understanding in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06537)
*Bodong Du,Bowen Liu,Yang Yu,Xinpeng Ding,Zhiheng Wu,Shuning Wang,Shuo Nie,Naiming Liu,Qifeng Chen,Yangqiu Song,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedHorizon基准，包含759小时完整临床手术视频和1253个基于证据的多选题，用于评估多模态大模型在长上下文医学视频理解中对稀疏证据的检索与多跳临床推理能力。最佳模型仅达41.1%准确率，揭示当前系统在完整流程理解上的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态大模型多聚焦于图像或短视频分析，而真实临床审查常需完整手术流程视频理解。现有基准假定关键证据已通过图像、短片段或预分割视频定位，未能充分测试"先检索再推理"这一核心问题。

Method: 构建MedHorizon基准：收集759小时全长临床手术视频（涵盖8个专科类别），设计1253个基于证据的多选题，每道题需要模型从极稀疏（平均仅0.166%证据帧）的噪声视频流中检索证据，再进行多跳临床推理。联合评估稀疏证据理解与多跳临床推理能力。

Result: 最佳模型（当前最优）仅达41.1%准确率。四项关键发现：(1)性能不随帧数增加可靠提升；(2)证据检索与临床解释为主要瓶颈；(3)瓶颈根源在于弱程序性推理与冗余下的注意力漂移；(4)通用采样方法仅能部分平衡局部细节与全局覆盖。

Conclusion: MedHorizon为评估多模态大模型在完整临床工作流中检索稀疏证据并进行推理提供了严格测试平台，表明当前系统远未实现鲁棒的完整流程医学视频理解。

Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced image understanding and short-video analysis, but real clinical review often requires full-procedure video understanding. Unlike general long videos, medical procedures contain highly redundant anatomical views, while decisive evidence is temporally sparse, spatially subtle, and context dependent. Existing benchmarks often assume this evidence has already been localized through images, short clips, or pre-segmented videos, leaving the retrieval-before-reasoning problem under-tested. We introduce MedHorizon, an in-the-wild benchmark for long-context medical video understanding. MedHorizon preserves 759 hours of full-length clinical procedures and provides 1,253 evidence-grounded multiple-choice questionsthat jointly evaluate sparse evidence understanding and multi-hop clinical reasoning. Its evidence is extremely sparse, with only 0.166% evidence frames on average, requiring models to search noisy procedural streams before interpreting and aggregating findings. We evaluate representative general-domain, medical-domain, and long-video MLLMs. The best model reaches only 41.1% accuracy, showing that current systems remain far from robust full-procedure understanding. Further analysis yields four key findings: performance does not scale reliably with more frames, evidence retrieval and clinical interpretation remain primary bottlenecks; these bottlenecks are rooted in weak procedural reasoning and attention drift under redundancy, and generic sampling methods only partially balances local detail with global coverage. MedHorizon provides a rigorous testbed for MLLMs that retrieve sparse evidence and reason over complete clinical workflows.

</details>


### [91] [Solving Minimal Problems Without Matrix Inversion Using FFT-Based Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06572)
*Haidong Wu,Snehal Bhayani,Janne Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于稀疏隐元结式的采样方法，无需矩阵求逆即可构建相机几何估计的求解器，通过逆快速傅里叶插值重建行列式多项式，并用Cramer法则恢复未知量，在多种最小问题上展现了良好的数值稳定性和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Gröbner基或结式的方法在求解相机几何估计中的多元多项式系统时，需要在线矩阵求逆，计算代价高且数值稳定性受限，因此需要一种无需矩阵求逆的高效替代方案。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏隐元结式构建求解器；2) 通过采样求值并用逆快速傅里叶变换插值重建隐变量的行列式多项式，避免符号展开；3) 求解该多项式得到隐变量后，通过识别秩-1亏损子矩阵并应用Cramer法则恢复其余未知量；4) 引入基于最大公约数的准则以在噪声下鲁棒地识别子矩阵。

Result: 在多种最小问题上的实验表明，所提求解器在数值稳定性和运行时间上表现良好，尤其是在小规模问题上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统Gröbner基和结式求解器提供了一种实用的替代方案，避免了矩阵求逆的瓶颈。

Abstract: Estimating camera geometry typically involves solving minimal problems formulated as systems of multivariate polynomial equations, which often pose computational challenges when using existing Gröbner-basis or resultant-based methods due to matrix inversion needed in the online solver. Here we propose a sampling-based, matrix inversion-free method that constructs the solvers using sparse hidden-variable resultants. The determinant polynomial in the hidden variable is efficiently reconstructed via inverse fast Fourier transform interpolation from sampled evaluations, avoiding symbolic expansion. Solving this polynomial yields the hidden variable, and the remaining unknowns are recovered by identifying rank-1 deficient submatrices and applying Cramer's rule. A greatest common divisor-based criterion ensures robust submatrix identification under noise. Experiments on diverse minimal problems demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves strong numerical stability and competitive runtime, particularly for small-scale problems, providing a practical alternative to traditional Gröbner-basis and resultant-based solvers.

</details>


### [92] [DINORANKCLIP: DINOv3 Distillation and Injection for Vision-Language Pretraining with High-Order Ranking Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06592)
*Shuyang Jiang,Nan Yu,Yiming Zhang,Zenghui Ding,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINORANKCLIP通过注入冻结的DINOv3教师、双分支轻量学生、多尺度融合模块以及高阶Plackett-Luce排序模型，同时解决了CLIP的对称InfoNCE损失忽略负样本排序和全局池化丢失细粒度局部结构两大缺陷，在72小时内以单节点8卡H100和Conceptual Captions 3M数据集完成训练，在细粒度和分布外评估上显著优于CLIP、CyCLIP、ALIP和RANKCLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP存在两个结构性缺陷：对称InfoNCE损失丢弃了批次内不匹配样本的相对排序信息，以及全局池化将视觉表示压缩为对细粒度局部结构不敏感的语义瓶颈。RANKCLIP仅部分解决了第一个问题（一阶排序），未触及第二个缺陷。

Method: 1) 注入冻结的DINOv3教师到对比主干，通过双分支轻量学生和多尺度融合模块（通道-空间注意力、自注意力精炼器、冲突感知门）实现；2) 引入高阶Plackett-Luce排序模型，在每位置效用中增加注意力参数化的成对和元组转移项，最优阶数R*=3。

Result: 在相同计算资源下，DINORANKCLIP在细粒度探测、模态差距分析、融合消融等实验中一致优于CLIP、CyCLIP、ALIP和RANKCLIP，在细粒度和分布外评估上取得最大相对增益。

Conclusion: 同时解决排序忽略和局部结构丢失两个问题能显著提升CLIP类模型在细粒度推理和分布泛化上的表现，高阶排序建模（R*=3）是有效折中。

Abstract: Contrastive language-image pretraining (CLIP) suffers from two structural weaknesses: the symmetric InfoNCE loss discards the relative ordering among unmatched in-batch pairs, and global pooling collapses the visual representation into a semantic bottleneck that is poorly sensitive to fine-grained local structure. RANKCLIP partially addresses the first issue with a list-wise Plackett-Luce ranking-consistency loss, but its model is strictly first-order and inherits the second weakness untouched. We propose DINORANKCLIP, a pretraining framework that addresses both jointly. Our principal contribution is injecting a frozen DINOv3 teacher into the contrastive trunk through a dual-branch lightweight student and a multi-scale fusion module with channel-spatial attention, a self-attention refiner, and a conflict-aware gate that preserves the cross-modal alignment up to first order. Complementarily, we introduce a high-order Plackett-Luce ranking model in which the per-position utility is augmented with attention-parameterised pairwise and tuple-wise transition terms; the family contains CLIP and RANKCLIP as nested zero-order and first-order special cases, and the optimal order on every benchmark is $R^*=3$. The full empirical study -- order sweep, Fine-grained Probe on five datasets, four-node Modality-Gap analysis, six-variant Fusion ablation -- fits in 72 hours on a single eight-GPU H100 node and trains entirely on Conceptual Captions 3M. DINORANKCLIP consistently outperforms CLIP, CyCLIP, ALIP, and RANKCLIP under matched compute, with the largest relative gains on the fine-grained and out-of-distribution evaluations that most directly stress local structural reasoning.

</details>


### [93] [DPM++: Dynamic Masked Metric Learning for Occluded Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06637)
*Lei Tan,Yingshi Luan,Pincong Zou,Pingyang Dai,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPM++，一种用于遮挡行人重识别的动态掩码度量学习框架。该方法通过输入自适应的动态掩码度量，在分类器-原型空间中结合CLIP两阶段监督，利用显著性引导的块转移策略合成逼真的遮挡样本，实现了遮挡场景下的鲁棒匹配，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人重识别方法在处理遮挡问题时，依赖额外的预训练模型估计可见部位或通过数据增强构造遮挡样本，但缺乏一个能够在真实遮挡模式下学习鲁棒的、可见性一致的匹配的统一框架。遮挡会移除判别性身体线索并引入背景和遮挡物的干扰，使全局度量学习不可靠。

Method: DPM++框架包含：1）输入自适应的动态掩码度量学习，为每个遮挡实例动态选择可靠的身份子空间；2）基于分类器-原型空间，引入CLIP两阶段监督方案，从文本分支学习ID级语义先验并迁移到分类器-原型空间；3）显著性引导的块转移策略，利用真实场景先验合成可控且逼真的遮挡样本；4）遮挡感知的样本配对和掩码引导优化。

Result: 在遮挡和整体行人重识别基准上，DPM++在整体和遮挡场景下均一致优于先前的最先进方法。

Conclusion: DPM++通过动态掩码度量学习框架有效解决了遮挡行人重识别中的可见性不一致问题，在真实遮挡模式下实现了鲁棒的匹配性能。

Abstract: Although person re-identification has made impressive progress, occlusion caused by obstacles remains an unsettled issue in real applications. The difficulty lies in the mismatch between incomplete occluded samples and holistic identity representations. Severe occlusion removes discriminative body cues and introduces interference from background clutter and occluders, making global metric learning unreliable. Existing methods mainly rely on extra pre-trained models to estimate visible parts for alignment or construct occluded samples via data augmentation, but still lack a unified framework that learns robust visibility-consistent matching under realistic occlusion patterns. In this paper, we propose DPM++, a Dynamic Masked Metric Learning framework for occluded person re-identification. DPM++ learns an input-adaptive masked metric that dynamically selects reliable identity subspaces for each occluded instance, enabling matching to emphasize visibility-consistent evidence while suppressing unreliable components. Built upon the classifier-prototype space, DPM++ introduces a CLIP-based two-stage supervision scheme, where ID-level semantic priors are learned from the text branch and transferred into the classifier-prototype space for dynamic masked matching. To strengthen the masked metric, we introduce a saliency-guided patch transfer strategy to synthesize controllable and photo-realistic occluded samples during training. Exploiting real scene priors, this strategy exposes the model to realistic partial observations and provides richer supervision than random erasing. In addition, occlusion-aware sample pairing and mask-guided optimization improve the stability and effectiveness of the framework. Experiments on occluded and holistic person re-identification benchmarks show that DPM++ consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods in both holistic and occlusion scenarios.

</details>


### [94] [Relit-LiVE: Relight Video by Jointly Learning Environment Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06658)
*Weiqing Xiao,Hong Li,Xiuyu Yang,Houyuan Chen,Wenyi Li,Tianqi Liu,Shaocong Xu,Chongjie Ye,Hao Zhao,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Relit-LiVE提出了一种无需相机位姿先验的视频重光照框架，通过引入原始参考图像和联合预测环境视频，实现物理一致、时间稳定的重光照效果，在合成和真实基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频扩散模型的重光照方法依赖于准确的内在分解（intrinsic decomposition），但真实世界视频的内在分解高度不可靠，导致重光照时出现外观失真、材质断裂和时序伪影累积等问题。

Method: 1) 在渲染过程中显式引入原始参考图像，恢复内在表征中丢失或损坏的场景线索；2) 提出环境视频预测（environment video prediction）方法，在单次扩散过程中同时生成重光照视频和每帧对应的环境贴图，实现几何-光照对齐，无需已知每帧相机位姿。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，Relit-LiVE在视频重光照和神经渲染任务中一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅实现高质量重光照，还自然支持场景级渲染、材质编辑、物体插入和流式视频重光照等多种下游应用。

Abstract: Recent advances have shown that large-scale video diffusion models can be repurposed as neural renderers by first decomposing videos into intrinsic scene representations and then performing forward rendering under novel illumination. While promising, this paradigm fundamentally relies on accurate intrinsic decomposition, which remains highly unreliable for real-world videos and often leads to distorted appearances, broken materials, and accumulated temporal artifacts during relighting. In this work, we present Relit-LiVE, a novel video relighting framework that produces physically consistent, temporally stable results without requiring prior knowledge of camera pose. Our key insight is to explicitly introduce raw reference images into the rendering process, enabling the model to recover critical scene cues that are inevitably lost or corrupted in intrinsic representations. Furthermore, we propose a novel environment video prediction formulation that simultaneously generates relit videos and per-frame environment maps aligned with each camera viewpoint in a single diffusion process. This joint prediction enforces strong geometric-illumination alignment and naturally supports dynamic lighting and camera motion, significantly improving physical consistency in video relighting while easing the requirement of known per-frame camera pose. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Relit-LiVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art video relighting and neural rendering methods across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Beyond relighting, our framework naturally supports a wide range of downstream applications, including scene-level rendering, material editing, object insertion, and streaming video relighting. The Project is available at https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE.

</details>


### [95] [BAMI: Training-Free Bias Mitigation in GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06664)
*Borui Zhang,Bo Zhang,Bo Wang,Wenzhao Zheng,Yuhao Cheng,Liang Tang,Yiqiang Yan,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BAMI方法，通过粗到细聚焦和候选选择两种操作，有效缓解了GUI grounding中因高分辨率导致的精度偏差和复杂界面导致的模糊偏差，在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上将TianXi-Action-7B模型的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%，且无需训练即可应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounding在复杂场景（如ScreenSpot-Pro基准）中性能不佳，现有模型常受限于高分辨率导致的精度偏差和复杂界面元素导致的模糊偏差。

Method: 首先利用掩码预测分布（MPD）归因方法识别误差来源（精度偏差和模糊偏差），然后提出偏差感知操作推理（BAMI），包含粗到细聚焦（coarse-to-fine focus）和候选选择（candidate selection）两种操作来缓解偏差，无需额外训练。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上，将TianXi-Action-7B模型的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%；消融实验验证了BAMI在不同参数配置下的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: BAMI方法在无需训练的条件下显著提升了多种GUI grounding模型的准确性，有效克服了精度偏差和模糊偏差问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: GUI grounding is a critical capability for enabling GUI agents to execute tasks such as clicking and dragging. However, in complex scenarios like the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark, existing models often suffer from suboptimal performance. Utilizing the proposed \textbf{Masked Prediction Distribution (MPD)} attribution method, we identify that the primary sources of errors are twofold: high image resolution (leading to precision bias) and intricate interface elements (resulting in ambiguity bias). To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI)}, which incorporates two key manipulations, coarse-to-fine focus and candidate selection, to effectively mitigate these biases. Our extensive experimental results demonstrate that BAMI significantly enhances the accuracy of various GUI grounding models in a training-free setting. For instance, applying our method to the TianXi-Action-7B model boosts its accuracy on the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark from 51.9\% to 57.8\%. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm the robustness of the BAMI approach across diverse parameter configurations, highlighting its stability and effectiveness. Code is available at https://github.com/Neur-IO/BAMI.

</details>


### [96] [ActCam: Zero-Shot Joint Camera and 3D Motion Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06667)
*Omar El Khalifi,Thomas Rossi,Oscar Fossey,Thibault Fouque,Ulysse Mizrahi,Philip Torr,Ivan Laptev,Fabio Pizzati,Baptiste Bellot-Gurlet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActCam是一种零样本视频生成方法，通过将驱动视频中的人物动作迁移到新场景，并支持逐帧控制相机内外参数，利用预训练的图像到视频扩散模型和两阶段条件调度（先深度+姿态，后仅姿态）实现无需训练的联合相机与动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术视频应用需要同时精细控制角色动作（表演）和相机轨迹（摄影），现有方法难以实现两者的联合控制。

Method: 基于预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，输入源视频和目标相机运动，生成几何一致的姿态和深度条件；采用两阶段采样调度：早期去噪步骤同时使用姿态和稀疏深度约束场景结构，后期丢弃深度仅用姿态引导以细化高频细节。

Result: 在多个基准上，相比纯姿态控制及其他姿态+相机方法，ActCam在相机遵循度和运动保真度上更优，在大视角变化下人类评估中更受偏好。

Conclusion: 精心设计的相机一致条件化与分阶段引导可以在无需训练的情况下实现强大的联合相机与运动控制。

Abstract: For artistic applications, video generation requires fine-grained control over both performance and cinematography, i.e., the actor's motion and the camera trajectory. We present ActCam, a zero-shot method for video generation that jointly transfers character motion from a driving video into a new scene and enables per-frame control of intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. ActCam builds on any pretrained image-to-video diffusion model that accepts conditioning in terms of scene depth and character pose. Given a source video with a moving character and a target camera motion, ActCam generates pose and depth conditions that remain geometrically consistent across frames. We then run a single sampling process with a two-phase conditioning schedule: early denoising steps condition on both pose and sparse depth to enforce scene structure, after which depth is dropped and pose-only guidance refines high-frequency details without over-constraining the generation. We evaluate ActCam on multiple benchmarks spanning diverse character motions and challenging viewpoint changes. We find that, compared to pose-only control and other pose and camera methods, ActCam improves camera adherence and motion fidelity, and is preferred in human evaluations, especially under large viewpoint changes. Our results highlight that careful camera-consistent conditioning and staged guidance can enable strong joint camera and motion control without training. Project page: https://elkhomar.github.io/actcam/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [AdaGATE: Adaptive Gap-Aware Token-Efficient Evidence Assembly for Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05245)
*Yilin Guo,Yinshan Wang,Yixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaGATE提出了一种无需训练的证据控制器，通过实体中心间隙追踪、目标微查询生成和基于效用的选择机制，在有限token预算下解决多跳RAG中证据缺失和冗余问题，在HotpotQA上实现了优于现有方法的证据F1分数，同时显著减少输入token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在多跳问答中面对噪声或冗余的检索证据时表现脆弱，且生成器的上下文窗口有限。已有的控制器要么加性扩展上下文，要么从固定top-k中选择，或优化相关性但没有显式修复缺失的桥接事实。

Method: 提出AdaGATE，一种无需训练的证据控制器，将证据选择建模为token约束下的修复问题，结合实体中心间隙追踪（识别缺失的桥接实体）、目标微查询生成（针对缺失信息生成子查询）以及基于效用的选择机制（平衡间隙覆盖率、佐证度、新颖性、冗余度和直接问题相关性）。

Result: 在HotpotQA的干净、冗余和噪声注入三种检索条件下评估，AdaGATE在所有设置下均取得了最佳证据F1，干净数据上达62.3%，冗余注入下达71.2%，同时输入token量比Adaptive-k少2.6倍。

Conclusion: 显式的间隙感知修复结合token高效的证据选择，能够提升多跳RAG在不完美检索条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) remains brittle on multi-hop questions in realistic deployment settings, where retrieved evidence may be noisy or redundant and only limited context can be passed to the generator. Existing controllers address parts of this problem, but typically either expand context additively, select from a fixed top-k set, or optimize relevance without explicitly repairing missing bridge facts. We propose AdaGATE, a training-free evidence controller for multi-hop RAG that frames evidence selection as a token-constrained repair problem. AdaGATE combines entity centric gap tracking, targeted micro-query generation, and a utility based selection mechanism that balances gap coverage, corroboration, novelty, redundancy, and direct question relevance. We evaluate AdaGATE on HotpotQA under clean, redundancy, and noise injected retrieval conditions. Across all three settings, AdaGATE achieves the best evidence F1 among the compared controllers, reaching 62.3% on clean data and 71.2% under redundancy injection, while using 2.6x fewer input tokens than Adaptive-k. These results suggest that explicit gap-aware repair, combined with token-efficient evidence selection, improves robustness in multi-hop RAG under imperfect retrieval. Our code and evaluation pipeline are available at https://github.com/eliguo/AdaGATE.

</details>


### [98] [Counterargument for Critical Thinking as Judged by AI and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05353)
*Tosin Adewumi,Marcus Liwicki,Foteini Simistira Liwicki,Lama Alkhaled,Hamam Mokayed,Esra Sümer-Arpak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究调查了36名学生在生成式AI背景下使用反驳论证进行批判性写作的效果，并通过6个评估标准（焦点、逻辑、内容、风格、正确性和参考文献）进行人工与AI评估。结果显示：学生的反驳论证包含逻辑等批判性思维关键要素；GenAI可大规模评估学生写作，且与人工评估基本一致（Gwet's AC2=0.33）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式AI（GenAI）日益普及的背景下，学生可能面临作弊和认知卸载的风险，因此需要探索如何利用AI促进而非削弱学生的批判性思维能力，特别是通过反驳论证的写作训练。

Method: 采用混合方法设计：36名学生从4个热门辩论选题中任选其一撰写论说文；使用6个既定评分标准（焦点、逻辑、内容、风格、正确性、参考文献）进行3次人工评估（2次同伴互评+1次教师评估）和6个前沿LLM评估，均采用5点李克特量表；对35份有效样本进行定量和定性分析。

Result: (1) 学生对AI生成内容的反驳论证包含了逻辑等批判性思维关键要素；(2) GenAI可基于清晰评分标准大规模评估学生写作，评估结果与人工评估基本一致，除一个模型外所有模型的Gwet's AC2一致性系数均为0.33。

Conclusion: 反驳论证写作是培养批判性思维的有效手段，GenAI既可作为学习工具也可作为评估工具，但需建立明确的评分标准；AI评估与人工评估具有可观的一致性，但仍存在模型间差异。

Abstract: This intervention study investigates the use of counterarguments in writing for critical thinking by students in the context of Generative AI (GenAI). This is especially as risks of cheating and cognitive offloading exist with the use of GenAI. We presented 36 students in a particular university course with 4 carefully selected thesis statements (from a set of popular debates) to write about anyone of them. We used six established rubrics (focus, logic, content, style, correctness and reference) to conduct three human assessments (two student peer-reviews and one experienced teacher) per writeup on a 5-point Likert scale for all the qualified samples (n) of 35 submissions (after disqualifying one for irregularity). Using the same rubrics and guidelines, we also assessed the submissions using six frontier LLMs as judges. Our mixed-method design included qualitative open-ended feedback per assessment and quantitative methods. The results reveal that (1) the students' self-written counterarguments to AI-generated content contains logic, among other things, which is a key component of critical thinking, and (2) GenAI can be successfully used at scale to assess students' written work, based on clear rubrics, and these assessments generally align with human assessments as shown with Gwets AC2 inter-rater reliability values of 0.33 for all the models except one.

</details>


### [99] [Generating Query-Focused Summarization Datasets from Query-Free Summarization Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05392)
*Yllias Chali,Deen Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于证据的查询生成模型，能从无查询数据集中自动生成查询关键词，并验证其用于查询聚焦摘要（QFS）任务的有效性。实验表明，使用生成的证据型查询生成的摘要与原始查询生成的摘要相比，能取得有竞争力的ROUGE分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模摘要数据集通常不包含查询，限制了其在查询聚焦摘要（QFS）任务中的应用。本文旨在解决如何从无查询数据集中自动生成基于证据的查询关键词，并验证其能否有效支持QFS任务。

Method: 提出一种基于证据的查询生成模型，从无查询数据集中自动生成查询。通过内在评估（比较生成查询与原始查询的相似度）和外在评估（使用不同预训练模型及SOTA QFS模型进行摘要生成）来验证该方法。

Result: 基于证据生成的查询所生成的摘要，在ROUGE评分上与使用原始查询生成的摘要相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 证据型查询生成方法能够有效支持查询聚焦摘要任务，为利用无查询数据集进行QFS提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets are widely used to perform summarization tasks, but they may not include queries alongside documents and summaries. In the search for suitable datasets for Query-Focused Summarization (QFS), we identify two research questions: Is it possible to automatically generate evidence-based query keywords from query-free datasets? Does evidence-based query generation support the QFS task? This paper proposes an evidence-based model to generate queries from query-free datasets. To evaluate our model intrinsically, we compare the similarity between the original queries and the system-generated queries of two QFS datasets. We also perform summarization tasks using different pre-trained models, as well as a state-of-the-art (SOTA) QFS model, to measure the extrinsic performance of our query generation approach. Experimental results indicate that summaries generated using evidence-based queries achieve competitive ROUGE scores compared to those generated from the original queries.

</details>


### [100] [SLAM: Structural Linguistic Activation Marking for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05443)
*Fabrice Harel-Canada,Amit Sahai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLAM是一种利用稀疏自编码器识别并因果干预残差流中编码语言结构的方向（如语态、时态、从句顺序）来实现水印嵌入的白盒方案，在不影响词汇采样和语义的前提下达到100%检测准确率，质量代价仅1-2奖励点，远低于KGW、EWD和Unigram的7.5-11.5点；其鲁棒性特征与传统方案互补：抗词级编辑但对改写语法结构敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方案通常通过偏置下一个token分布来嵌入水印，导致可检测的文本质量损失，亟需一种不牺牲文本质量即可实现可靠水印检测的方法。

Method: SLAM利用稀疏自编码器识别残差流中编码语言结构（语态、时态、从句顺序等）的方向，在生成时对这些方向进行因果干预（因果操纵），从而将水印嵌入文本的结构几何特征中，而不影响词汇采样和语义。

Result: 在Gemma-2 2B和9B模型上，SLAM实现100%检测准确率，质量代价仅1-2奖励点，远低于对比方法KGW、EWD和Unigram的7.5-11.5点；自然度和多样性保持在接近无水印水平；对词级编辑鲁棒，但对改写语法结构敏感。

Conclusion: SLAM证明了一种无需牺牲文本质量即可嵌入可检测水印的可行路径，其鲁棒性特征与传统的token分布方法是互补的，为LLM水印提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: LLM watermarks must be detectable without compromising text quality, yet most existing schemes bias the next-token distribution and pay for detection with measurable quality loss. We present SLAM (Structural Linguistic Activation Marking), a novel white-box watermarking scheme that sidesteps this cost by writing the mark into structural geometry rather than token frequencies: sparse autoencoders identify residual-stream directions encoding linguistic structure (e.g., voice, tense, clause order), and we causally steer those directions at generation time, leaving lexical sampling and semantics unconstrained. On Gemma-2 2B and 9B, SLAM achieves 100% detection accuracy with a quality cost of only 1-2 reward points - compared to 7.5-11.5 for KGW, EWD, and Unigram - with naturalness and diversity preserved at near-unwatermarked levels across both models. The trade-off is a complementary robustness profile: SLAM resists word-level edits but is vulnerable to paraphrase that restructures syntax (at a quality cost), the converse of token-distribution methods.

</details>


### [101] [Chainwash: Multi-Step Rewriting Attacks on Diffusion Language Model Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05503)
*Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Akif Islam,Nadim Mahmud,Md. Ekramul Hamid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了对扩散语言模型生成的文本进行多次重写（链式重写）对统计水印检测的影响。实验表明，经过5次链式重写后，水印检测率从原始的87.9%降至4.86%，即94.76%的原始可检测文本不再被标记，且该结论在四种不同重写模型和五种重写风格下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方案主要针对自回归生成模型设计，扩散语言模型（如LLaDA）的任意顺序去噪生成方式使得传统水印无法直接应用。虽然Gloaguen等人提出了针对扩散模型的水印方案并报告了99%以上的真阳性检测率，但该方案在文本被多次重写后的鲁棒性尚未被研究。

Method: 使用LLaDA 8B Instruct模型，在5个WaterBench领域生成1,605个约300词的水印文本。使用4个开源语言模型（1.5B-8B参数，均不知晓水印密钥）进行5种风格（释义、人性化、简化、学术、摘要扩展）的重写，每种风格最多链式重写5次，共产生160,500个重写文本。在标准显著性阈值下检测水印。

Result: 原始输出的水印检测率为87.9%。单次重写后检测率降至14%-41%（取决于重写器和风格）。5次链式重写后检测率降至4.86%，即94.76%的原始可检测文本无法再被检测到。3次重写后，检测器分数已从水印基线向零分布下降了86%。

Conclusion: 多次链式重写是比单次重写更强的攻击方式，该结论在四种重写器和五种重写风格下均成立。现有针对扩散语言模型的水印方案在面对多次重写时鲁棒性不足。

Abstract: Statistical watermarking is a common approach for verifying whether text was written by a language model. Most existing schemes assume autoregressive generation, where tokens are produced left to right and contextual hashing is well defined. Diffusion language models generate text by denoising tokens in arbitrary order, so these schemes cannot be applied directly. A recent watermark by Gloaguen et al. addresses this gap for LLaDA 8B Instruct and reports true positive detection above 99%. This paper studies what happens when watermarked text is rewritten not once but several times. Using the same watermark configuration, 1,605 watermarked completions of about 300 tokens each are produced across five WaterBench domains. Each completion is rewritten by four open weight language models, from 1.5B to 8B parameters, none of which know the watermark key. Five rewrite styles are tested: paraphrase, humanize, simplify, academic, and summarize expand. Each style is chained for up to five hops, producing 160,500 rewritten texts in total. The watermark is detected on 87.9% of the original outputs at the standard significance threshold. After a single rewrite, detection falls to between 14% and 41% depending on the rewriter and style. After five chained rewrites, detection falls to 4.86%, meaning 94.76% of the originally detected texts are no longer flagged. After three rewrites, the detector score has dropped 86% of the way from its watermarked baseline toward the null distribution. Repeated rewriting is therefore a much stronger attack than a single rewrite, and the result holds across all four rewriters tested.

</details>


### [102] [A Few Good Clauses: Comparing LLMs vs Domain-Trained Small Language Models on Structured Contract Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05532)
*Nicole Lincoln,Nick Whitehouse,Jaron Mar,Rivindu Perera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了领域训练的小语言模型（SLM）在结构化合同提取任务中能否以极低成本超越前沿大语言模型（LLM）。实验发现，自托管的法律领域混合专家模型 Olava Extract 在聚合性能上最强（宏F1 0.812，微F1 0.842），同时推理成本降低78%-97%，且幻觉和虚假提取更少。结果表明，高性能、可媲美人类的法务AI不再需要最大的外部托管模型，挑战了企业AI必须依赖更大模型、巨额基础设施和中心化服务商的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证领域训练的小语言模型（SLM）是否能在结构化合同提取任务中，以远低于前沿大语言模型的成本取得更优或可比的性能，从而挑战当前企业AI依赖超大模型和高昂基础设施的主流范式。

Method: 以自托管的法律领域混合专家（MoE）模型 Olava Extract 为SLM代表，与五个前沿大语言模型进行对比实验，评估其在结构化合同提取任务上的宏F1、微F1、精确率以及推理成本等指标。

Result: Olava Extract 在聚合性能上最强，宏F1为0.812，微F1为0.842，推理成本相比前沿模型降低78%至97%，同时精确率最高，幻觉和虚假提取最少。

Conclusion: 高性能、可媲美人类的法务AI不再需要最大规模的外部托管模型；该发现挑战了企业AI能力必须依赖更大模型、巨额基础设施支出和中心化托管商的普遍假设。

Abstract: This paper evaluates whether a domain trained Small Language Model (SLM) can outperform frontier Large Language Models on structured contract extraction at radically lower cost. We test Olava Extract, a self hosted legal domain Mixture of Experts model, against five frontier models.
  Olava Extract achieved the strongest aggregate performance in the study, with a macro F1 of 0.812 and a micro F1 of 0.842, while reducing inference cost by 78% to 97% compared with the frontier models tested. It also achieved the highest precision scores, producing fewer hallucinated and unsupported extractions, an important distinction in legal workflows where hallucinations create operational risk and downstream review burden.
  The findings shows that high performing, human comparable legal AI no longer requires the largest externally hosted models. More broadly, they challenge the assumption that commercially valuable enterprise AI capability must remain tied to ever larger models, massive infrastructure expenditure, and centrally hosted providers.

</details>


### [103] [The Cost of Context: Mitigating Textual Bias in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05594)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型在引入RAG后会出现"再污染"现象——即使提供完全正确的上下文，模型也会放弃原本正确的预测。通过注意力机制诊断，发现这是由视觉盲区和位置偏见共同导致的双重注意力坍缩。作者提出了无需参数训练的BAIR方法，在医疗、公平性和地理空间基准上有效恢复了多模态基础能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RAG被广泛用于缓解MLLM的幻觉问题，但引入外部文档可能在实例层面隐藏严重的失败模式。本文旨在识别并形式化"再污染"现象，揭示RAG在某些情况下反而使模型性能下降的机制。

Method: 通过诊断内部注意力矩阵，发现"再污染"由双重注意力坍缩驱动：(1) 视觉盲区——系统性地抑制视觉注意力质量和锐度；(2) 结构性的位置偏见——强迫模型优先关注边界标记而非语义相关性。提出BAIR方法，这是一种无需参数、推理阶段的框架，通过恢复视觉显著性并施加位置感知惩罚来修复文本干扰。

Result: 在医疗事实性、社会公平性和地理空间基准测试中，BAIR成功恢复了多模态基础能力，改善了诊断可靠性，且无需模型重新训练或微调。

Conclusion: 揭示了"成功的幻觉"现象——许多看似正确的RAG结果仅仅是位置巧合，模型文本复制偏见恰好与真实答案位置对齐。BAIR作为一种轻量级、无需训练的干预方法，有效解决了RAG引入的多模态基础能力退化问题。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly integrated with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to mitigate hallucinations, the introduction of external documents can conceal severe failure modes at the instance level. We identify and formalize the phenomenon of recorruption, where the introduction of even perfectly accurate "oracle" context causes a capable model to abandon an initially correct prediction. Through a mechanistic diagnosis of internal attention matrices, we show that recorruption is driven by a two-fold attentional collapse: (1) visual blindness, characterized by the systemic suppression of visual attention mass ($M_{vis}$) and sharpness ($S_{vis}$), and (2) a structural positional bias that forces the model to prioritize boundary tokens over semantic relevance. Our analysis reveals an Illusion of Success, demonstrating that many seemingly correct RAG outcomes are merely positional coincidences where the model's textual copying bias happens to align with the ground-truth location. To address these vulnerabilities, we propose Bottleneck Attention Intervention for Recovery (BAIR), a parameter-free, inference-time framework that restores visual saliency and applies position-aware penalties to textual distractors. Across medical factuality, social fairness, and geospatial benchmarks, BAIR successfully restores multimodal grounding and improves diagnostic reliability without requiring model retraining or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [104] [When2Speak: A Dataset for Temporal Participation and Turn-Taking in Multi-Party Conversations for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05626)
*Vihaan Nama,Shreya Mendi,Zian Ye,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出When2Speak，一个用于学习多轮对话中干预时机的地基合成数据集及四阶段生成流程。通过215,000+样本训练，SFT使模型Macro F1提升最高120%，但存在过度保守问题（MIR约0.50）。采用非对称奖励塑造的强化学习将MIR降至0.186-0.218，召回率从0.479提升至0.78-0.81，证明时间参与是可训练的对话智能维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成合适上下文响应方面表现出色，但在多轮对话中缺乏对"何时该说话"的判断能力，随意回会导致频繁打断和对话连贯性下降。

Method: 构建When2Speak数据集（215,000+样本，16,000个对话，2-6人），采用四阶段生成流程：现实世界基础、结构化增强、受控转录合成和微调就绪监督。先进行SFT训练，再使用非对称奖励塑造的强化学习优化。

Result: SFT平均Macro F1提升60%，最大提升120%；但MIR平均为0.50（过度保守）。强化学习后MIR降至0.186-0.218，召回率从0.479提升至0.78-0.81。

Conclusion: 时间参与是对话智能中一个独立可训练的维度，地基合成数据为LLMs在多轮交互中更自然恰当地参与提供了可扩展的有效路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at generating contextually appropriate responses but remain poorly calibrated for multi-party conversations, where deciding when to speak is as critical as what to say. In such settings, naively responding at every turn leads to excessive interruptions and degraded conversational coherence. We introduce When2Speak, a grounded synthetic dataset and four-stage generation pipeline for learning intervention timing in group interactions. The dataset comprises over 215,000 examples derived from 16,000 conversations involving 2-6 speakers, spanning diverse conversational styles, tones, and participant dynamics, and explicitly modeling SPEAK vs. SILENT decisions at each turn. Our pipeline combines real-world grounding, structured augmentation, controlled transcript synthesis, and fine-tuning-ready supervision, and is fully open-sourced to support reproducibility and adaptation to domain-specific conversational norms. Across multiple model families, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on When2Speak significantly outperforms zero-shot baselines (e.g., the average Macro F1 increase across 4B+ parameter models was 60%, with the largest increase being 120%). However, SFT-trained models remain systematically over-conservative, missing nearly half of warranted interventions as seen through the Missed Intervention Rate (MIR), which was on average 0.50 and is noticed even at larger model sizes. To address this limitation, we apply reinforcement learning with asymmetric reward shaping, which reduces MIR to 0.186-0.218 and increases recall from 0.479 to 0.78-0.81. Our findings establish that temporal participation is a distinct and trainable dimension of conversational intelligence, and that grounded synthetic data provides an effective and scalable pathway for enabling LLMs to participate more naturally and appropriately in multi-party interactions.

</details>


### [105] [One Turn Too Late: Response-Aware Defense Against Hidden Malicious Intent in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05630)
*Xinjie Shen,Rongzhe Wei,Peizhi Niu,Haoyu Wang,Ruihan Wu,Eli Chien,Bo Li,Pin-Yu Chen,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TurnGate方法，通过检测多轮对话中最早可造成危害的转折点（harm-enabling closure point），实现精确的逐轮恶意意图检测，并构建了包含分支攻击路径、良性硬负样本及标注的MTID数据集。TurnGate在有害意图检测上显著优于现有基线，同时保持低过度拒绝率，并具备跨领域、攻击流水线及目标模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中隐藏的恶意意图对部署的大型语言模型构成日益严重的威胁。攻击者可将恶意目标分散到多个看似良性的对话轮次中，现有安全对齐和外部护栏对此类攻击仍显脆弱。亟需一种能在最早可造成危害的轮次进行精确干预的检测方法。

Method: 1) 定义"harm-enabling closure point"作为最早使累积交互足以实现有害行为的对话轮次；2) 构建Multi-Turn Intent Dataset (MTID)，包含分支攻击展开、匹配的良性硬负样本及最早危害启用轮次的标注；3) 提出TurnGate逐轮监控器，利用MTID训练实现精确的轮次级干预。

Result: TurnGate在有害意图检测上大幅优于现有基线，同时保持低过度拒绝率；具备跨领域、攻击流水线和目标模型的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过精确定位最早危害启用轮次并进行轮次级干预，能有效检测多轮对话中的隐藏恶意意图，且MTID数据集对训练和评估此类方法具有重要价值。

Abstract: Hidden malicious intent in multi-turn dialogue poses a growing threat to deployed large language models (LLMs). Rather than exposing a harmful objective in a single prompt, increasingly capable attackers can distribute their intent across multiple benign-looking turns. Recent studies show that even modern commercial models with advanced guardrails remain vulnerable to such attacks despite advances in safety alignment and external guardrails. In this work, we address this challenge by detecting the earliest turn at which delivering the candidate response would make the accumulated interaction sufficient to enable harmful action. This objective requires precise turn-level intervention that identifies the harm-enabling closure point while avoiding premature refusal of benign exploratory conversations. To further support training and evaluation, we construct the Multi-Turn Intent Dataset (MTID), which contains branching attack rollouts, matched benign hard negatives, and annotations of the earliest harm-enabling turns. We show that MTID helps enable a turn-level monitor TurnGate, which substantially outperforms existing baselines in harmful-intent detection while maintaining low over-refusal rates. TurnGate further generalizes across domains, attacker pipelines, and target models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/TurnGate.

</details>


### [106] [XL-SafetyBench: A Country-Grounded Cross-Cultural Benchmark for LLM Safety and Cultural Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05662)
*Dasol Choi,Eugenia Kim,Jaewon Noh,Sang Seo,Eunmi Kim,Myunggyo Oh,Yunjin Park,Brigitta Jesica Kartono,Josef Pichlmeier,Helena Berndt,Sai Krishna Mendu,Glenn Johannes Tungka,Özlem Gökçe,Suresh Gehlot,Katherine Pratt,Amanda Minnich,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XL-SafetyBench是一个包含5,500个测试用例的多语言安全评测基准，覆盖10个国家-语言对，包含对抗性越狱和文化敏感性两个子集。通过引入ASR、NSR和CSR三个指标，评估了10个前沿模型和27个本地模型，发现越狱鲁棒性与文化意识在主流模型中无耦合关系，而本地模型的"安全"表现实为生成失败而非真正对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准以英语为中心，依赖翻译方式无法捕捉特定国家的危害，且缺乏区分文化嵌入敏感性与通用危害的能力。

Method: 构建了5,500个测试用例（10个国家-语言对），含越狱基准和文化基准两个子集。采用多阶段流水线：LLM辅助发现、自动验证门控、每国双独立母语标注者。引入三个指标：ASR（攻击成功率）、NSR（中性安全率）、CSR（文化敏感率）。

Result: 前沿模型中越狱鲁棒性与文化意识无耦合关系；本地模型呈现近线性ASR-NSR权衡（r=-0.81），其表面安全反映的是生成失败而非真正对齐。

Conclusion: XL-SafetyBench能够实现更细致、跨文化的安全评测，复合安全分数会掩盖不同轴向上的变化。

Abstract: Current LLM safety benchmarks are predominantly English-centric and often rely on translation, failing to capture country-specific harms. Moreover, they rarely evaluate a model's ability to detect culturally embedded sensitivities as distinct from universal harms. We introduce XL-SafetyBench. a suite of 5,500 test cases across 10 country-language pairs, comprising a Jailbreak Benchmark of country-grounded adversarial prompts and a Cultural Benchmark where local sensitivities are embedded within innocuous requests. Each item is constructed via a multi-stage pipeline that combines LLM-assisted discovery, automated validation gates, and dual independent native-speaker annotators per country. To distinguish principled refusal from comprehension failure, we evaluate Attack Success Rate (ASR) alongside two complementary metrics we introduce: Neutral-Safe Rate (NSR) and Cultural Sensitivity Rate (CSR). Evaluating 10 frontier and 27 local LLMs reveals two key findings. First, jailbreak robustness and cultural awareness do not show a coupled relationship among frontier models, so a composite safety score obscures per-axis variation. Second, local models exhibit a near-linear ASR-NSR trade-off (r = -0.81), indicating that their apparent safety reflects generation failure rather than genuine alignment. XL-SafetyBench enables more nuanced, cross-cultural safety evaluation in the multilingual era.

</details>


### [107] [Decomposing the Basic Abilities of Large Language Models: Mitigating Cross-Task Interference in Multi-Task Instruct-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05676)
*Bing Wang,Ximing Li,Changchun Li,Jinjin Chi,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BADIT方法，通过将LLM参数分解为正交的高奇异值LoRA专家（代表基本能力），并在训练中通过球面聚类动态维持正交性，以缓解多任务指令微调中的跨任务干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多任务指令微调存在跨任务干扰（cross-task interference），即不同任务的梯度在共享参数上冲突。已有方法（如任务特定神经元选择、混合专家模型）因仍有大量参数被不同任务共享，无法彻底消除该问题。

Method: 1）发现参数被一致共激活，自然形成基组；2）将LLM参数分解为正交的高奇异值LoRA专家，每个专家代表一种基本能力；3）通过球面聚类（spherical clustering）对rank-1组件进行动态正交性约束训练。

Result: 在SuperNI基准测试上对6个LLM进行实验，BADIT优于现有SOTA方法，并有效降低了跨任务干扰程度。

Conclusion: 将任务视为基本能力的线性组合，通过参数分解和正交约束可以更有效地缓解多任务指令微调中的跨任务干扰问题。

Abstract: Recently, the prominent performance of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by multi-task instruct-tuning.
